A. STABLE IDENTITY AND SOURCE EVIDENCE

Clause / section / page: 4.2.3 / Part II-A Chapter 4 - Sewage / 43
Source URL: https://wwwcdn.imo.org/localresources/en/KnowledgeCentre/IndexofIMOResolutions/MSCResolutions/MSC.385%2894%29.pdf
Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/MSC.385(94).pdf

Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-IMO-119-20260915T114643706487Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
Category A and B ships that operate in areas of ice concentrations exceeding 1/10 for extended periods of time, may only discharge sewage using an approved sewage treatment plant certified by the Administration to meet the operational requirements in regulation 9.1.1 or 9.2.1 of MARPOL Annex IV. Such discharges shall be subject to approval by the Administration.
Normalized requirement:
IF ship_category IN {'A','B'} AND ice_concentration_tenths > 1 AND extended_period_operation = true AND sewage_discharge_occurs = true, THEN approved_sewage_treatment_plant_used = true, MARPOL_IV_9_1_1_or_9_2_1_certification_status = compliant, and administration_discharge_approval_status = approved.
Required inputs: Ship category, operating ice data, treatment-plant certificate, and discharge approval

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO_119_Sewage_Discharge_Conditional>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;

    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipCategory ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:polarShipCategoryA
            nltl:polarShipCategoryB
            nltl:polarShipCategoryC
        ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A canonical ship category is required."
    ] ;

    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceConcentration ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "Ice concentration, when provided, must be a unitless quantity."
    ] ;

    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:extendedPeriodOperation ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:message "Extended-period operation status, when provided, must be boolean."
    ] ;

    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:sewageDischargeOccurs ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:message "Sewage-discharge status, when provided, must be boolean."
    ] ;

    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:approvedSewageTreatmentPlantUsed ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:message "Approved sewage treatment plant usage status, when provided, must be boolean."
    ] ;

    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:marpolAnnexIvRegulation9Point1Point1Or9Point2Point1CertificationStatus ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:evidenceStateApproved
            nltl:evidenceStateDraft
            nltl:evidenceStateExpired
            nltl:evidenceStateRejected
            nltl:evidenceStateRevoked
            nltl:evidenceStateSubmitted
            nltl:evidenceStateUnderReview
        ) ;
        sh:message "MARPOL Annex IV certification status, when provided, must use a canonical evidence state."
    ] ;

    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:administrationDischargeApprovalStatus ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:evidenceStateApproved
            nltl:evidenceStateDraft
            nltl:evidenceStateExpired
            nltl:evidenceStateRejected
            nltl:evidenceStateRevoked
            nltl:evidenceStateSubmitted
            nltl:evidenceStateUnderReview
        ) ;
        sh:message "Administration discharge approval status, when provided, must use a canonical evidence state."
    ] ;

    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "For Category A or B ships with ice concentration above 1/10 operating for extended periods and discharging sewage, an approved sewage treatment plant, MARPOL Annex IV regulation 9.1.1 or 9.2.1 certification, and Administration-approved discharge status are required." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:shipCategory ?category .
                $this nltl:iceConcentration ?iceQuantity .
                ?iceQuantity qudt:numericValue ?iceValue .
                ?iceQuantity qudt:unit unit:UNITLESS .
                $this nltl:extendedPeriodOperation ?extended .
                $this nltl:sewageDischargeOccurs ?discharge .

                FILTER (
                    ?category IN (
                        nltl:polarShipCategoryA,
                        nltl:polarShipCategoryB
                    )
                    && ?iceValue > "1"^^xsd:decimal
                    && ?extended = true
                    && ?discharge = true
                )

                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:approvedSewageTreatmentPlantUsed ?approvedValue .
                    FILTER (?approvedValue = true)
                }

                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:marpolAnnexIvRegulation9Point1Point1Or9Point2Point1CertificationStatus
                        nltl:evidenceStateApproved .
                }

                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:administrationDischargeApprovalStatus
                        nltl:evidenceStateApproved .
                }
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship category must be recorded as Category A, B, or C."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceConcentration ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "Ice concentration must be supplied as a single dimensionless quantity."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:extendedPeriodOperation ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:sewageDischargeOccurs ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "For a Category A or B ship operating for an extended period in ice concentration exceeding 1/10 and discharging sewage, an approved sewage treatment plant and Administration approval are required." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:shipCategory ?category .
                FILTER (?category IN (
                    nltl:polarShipCategoryA,
                    nltl:polarShipCategoryB
                )) .

                $this nltl:iceConcentration ?iceQuantity .
                ?iceQuantity qudt:numericValue ?iceValue ;
                              qudt:unit unit:UNITLESS .
                FILTER (?iceValue > 1) .

                $this nltl:extendedPeriodOperation true ;
                      nltl:sewageDischargeOccurs true .

                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:approvedSewageTreatmentPlantUsed true ;
                          nltl:administrationDischargeApprovalStatus nltl:evidenceStateApproved .
                }
            }
        """
    ] .

```

```turtle
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:shipCategory ?category .
                FILTER (?category IN (
                    nltl:polarShipCategoryA,
                    nltl:polarShipCategoryB
                ))

                $this nltl:iceConcentration ?ice .

                     qudt:unit unit:UNITLESS .
```

Original source text:
If the structure is evaluated based on non-linear calculation methods, the following are to be considered: (1) The analysis is to reliably capture buckling and plastic deformation of the structure (2) The acceptance criteria are to ensure a suitable margin against fracture and major buckling and yielding causing significant loss of stiffness (3) Permanent lateral and out-of plane deformation of considered member are to be minor relative to the relevant structural dimensions

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .

@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:I2_065_ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasCalculationCase ;
        sh:class nltl:calculationCase
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:fractureMargin ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:majorBucklingMargin ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;

                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]

    ] ;

                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:bucklingResponse ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:plasticDeformation ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "A ship with a nonlinear calculation case must provide fracture, major-buckling, and yielding margins." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasCalculationCase ?case .
                ?case nltl:calculationMethod nltl:nonlinearCalculationMethodValue .
                FILTER (
                    NOT EXISTS { $this nltl:fractureMargin ?fracture }
                    || NOT EXISTS { $this nltl:majorBucklingMargin ?buckling }
                    || NOT EXISTS { $this nltl:yieldingMargin ?yielding }
                )
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "A ship with a nonlinear calculation case must provide explicit assessments that the analysis reliably captures buckling and plastic deformation of the structure." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasCalculationCase ?case .
                ?case nltl:calculationMethod nltl:nonlinearCalculationMethodValue .
                FILTER (
                    NOT EXISTS { $this nltl:bucklingResponse ?bucklingResponse }
                    || NOT EXISTS { $this nltl:plasticDeformation ?plasticDeformation }
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>

                $this nltl:calculationMethod nltl:nonlinearCalculationMethodValue .

                        $this nltl:minorPermanentDeformationAssessmentSatisfied true

                    || NOT EXISTS { $this nltl:relevantStructuralDimension ?dimension }
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
                    ] ;

                        sh:path nltl:relevantStructuralDimension ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:node [
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                                sh:minCount 1 ;
                                sh:maxCount 1
                            ] ;
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                                sh:hasValue unit:MilliM ;
                                sh:minCount 1 ;
                                sh:maxCount 1
                            ]
                        ]
                    ]
                ]
                [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:calculationMethod ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:hasValue nltl:linearCalculationMethodValue
                    ]
                ]
                [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:calculationMethod ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:hasValue nltl:directCalculationMethodValue
                    ]
                ]
            )
        ]
    ] ;

    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:fractureMargin ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;

    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:majorBucklingMargin ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;

    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:yieldingMargin ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;

    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "A ship with a nonlinear calculation case must provide fracture, major buckling, and yielding margins." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasCalculationCase ?case .
                ?case nltl:calculationMethod nltl:nonlinearCalculationMethodValue .
                FILTER (
                    NOT EXISTS { $this nltl:fractureMargin ?fractureMargin }
                    || NOT EXISTS { $this nltl:majorBucklingMargin ?majorBucklingMargin }
                    || NOT EXISTS { $this nltl:yieldingMargin ?yieldingMargin }
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

Clause / section / page: 6.6.5.4 / Acceptability criterion for static loads / 60
Source URL: https://www.finlex.fi/en/authorities/regulations/traficom-transport-maritime/2021/47238
Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/TRAFICOM.pdf
Local source PDF SHA-256: 8ab845347770c253433b8bad9b1fbe5d6ac71b3d07a1a28dae8a32cfde732ddc
Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-TRF-118-20260915T140712356440Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
The stresses on the thruster must be calculated for the extreme once-in-a-lifetime loads described in section 6.6.5. The nominal von Mises stresses on the thruster body must have a safety margin of 1.3 against the yielding strength of the material. At areas of local stress concentrations, stresses must have a safety margin of 1.0 against yielding. The slewing bearing, bolt connections and other components must be able to maintain operability without incurring damage that requires repair when subject to the loads given in sections 6.6.5.2 and 6.6.5.3 multiplied by a safety factor of 1.3.
Normalized requirement:
The stresses on the thruster must be calculated for the extreme once-in-a-lifetime loads described in section 6.6.5. The nominal von Mises stresses on the thruster body must have a safety margin of 1.3 against the yielding strength of the material. At areas of local stress concentrations, stresses must have a safety margin of 1.0 against yielding. The slewing bearing, bolt connections and other components must be able to maintain operability without incurring damage that requires repair when subject to the loads given in sections 6.6.5.2 and 6.6.5.3 multiplied by a safety factor of 1.3.
Required inputs: No explicit data variable identified

```turtle
    ] ;

        ] ;

                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "A local stress concentration value with a dimensionless numeric value is required."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasFactoredExtremeLoadOperabilityAssessment ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class nltl:factoredExtremeLoadOperabilityAssessment ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:assessedThrusterComponent ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class nltl:shipComponent ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:hasMaterialProperties ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:node [
                            sh:class nltl:materialProperties ;
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path nltl:componentMaterialYieldStrength ;
                                sh:minCount 1 ;

                                        sh:minCount 1 ;

                                    ] ;
                                    sh:property [

                                ] ;
                                sh:message "Component material yield strength in MPa is required."
                            ]
                        ]
                    ]
                ] ;
                sh:message "The assessment must identify its assessed thruster component."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:assessedFactoredExtremeLoad ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
```

Original source text:
The ship is discharging comminuted and disinfected sewage in accordance with regulation 11.1.1 of MARPOL Annex IV at a distance of more than three nautical miles from any ice-shelf or fast ice and shall be as far as practicable from areas of ice concentration exceeding 1/10.
Normalized requirement:
IF sewage_discharge_type = comminuted_and_disinfected, THEN MARPOL_IV_11_1_1_compliance_status = compliant AND distance_to_nearest_ice_shelf_or_fast_ice_nm > 3; record distance from areas with ice_concentration_tenths > 1.
Required inputs: Discharge type, MARPOL compliance, position, and ice-condition data

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix g: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

g:IMO_115_ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:sewageDischargeType ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A sewage discharge type is required to determine applicability of the conditional rule."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "When sewage discharge is comminuted and disinfected, MARPOL Annex IV regulation 11.1.1 compliance, the distance to nearest ice shelf or fast ice, nearby ice concentration, and the distinct distance from areas exceeding 1/10 ice concentration must be recorded and conform to the specified conditions." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:sewageDischargeType ?dischargeType .
                FILTER (STR(?dischargeType) = "comminuted_and_disinfected")

                {
                    FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:marpolAnnexIvRegulation11Point1Point1ComplianceStatus true
                    }
                }
                UNION
                {
                    FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:distanceToNearestIceShelfOrFastIce ?nearestDistance .
                        ?nearestDistance rdf:type qudt:QuantityValue ;
                            qudt:numericValue ?nearestValue ;
                            qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MI_N> .
                        FILTER (
                            datatype(?nearestValue) = xsd:decimal &&
                            ?nearestValue > "3"^^xsd:decimal
                        )
                    }
                }
                UNION
                {
                    FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:nearbyIceConcentration ?nearbyConcentration .
                        ?nearbyConcentration rdf:type qudt:QuantityValue ;
                            qudt:numericValue ?nearbyValue ;
                            qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> .
                        FILTER (
                            datatype(?nearbyValue) = xsd:decimal &&
                            ?nearbyValue > "0.1"^^xsd:decimal
                        )
                    }
                }
                UNION
                {
                    FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:distanceFromAreaWithIceConcentrationAboveOneTenth ?areaDistance .
                        ?areaDistance rdf:type qudt:QuantityValue ;
                            qudt:numericValue ?areaDistanceValue ;
                            qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MI_N> .
                        FILTER (datatype(?areaDistanceValue) = xsd:decimal)
                    }
                }
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:IMO115Shape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:or (
        gen:IMO115ApplicableCase
        gen:IMO115NotApplicableCase
    ) .

gen:IMO115ApplicableCase a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:sewageDischargeType ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:hasValue "comminuted_and_disinfected" ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The sewage discharge type must be comminuted and disinfected when this applicability branch is selected."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:comminutedAndDisinfected ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:marpolAnnexIvRegulation11Point1Point1ComplianceStatus ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Compliance with MARPOL Annex IV regulation 11.1.1 must be recorded as true."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:distanceToNearestIceShelfOrFastIce ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minExclusive "3"^^xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:MI_N ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The ship must be more than three nautical miles from the nearest ice shelf or fast ice."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:nearbyIceConcentration ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minExclusive "1"^^xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The recorded nearby ice concentration must identify areas exceeding one tenth."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:distanceFromAreaWithIceConcentrationAboveOneTenth ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:MI_N ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The distance from areas with ice concentration exceeding one tenth must be recorded."
    ] .

gen:IMO115NotApplicableCase a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:sewageDischargeType ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:comminutedAndDisinfected ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:hasValue false ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "The non-applicable branch cannot use the comminuted-and-disinfected sewage discharge type." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:sewageDischargeType ?dischargeType .
                FILTER (str(?dischargeType) = "comminuted_and_disinfected")
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
gen:I2_066_ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasWeld ;
        sh:class nltl:weld ;
        sh:node gen:I2_066_WeldScopeShape ;
        sh:message "Each linked weld must provide its ice-strengthened-area scope and satisfy the applicable weld-type requirement."
    ] .

gen:I2_066_WeldScopeShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:withinIceStrengthenedArea ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each weld must state whether it is within an ice-strengthened area."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:withinIceStrengthenedArea ;
                sh:hasValue true
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:weldType ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue nltl:doubleContinuousWeld ;
                sh:message "A weld within an ice-strengthened area must be of the double continuous type."
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:withinIceStrengthenedArea ;
                sh:hasValue false
            ]
        ]
    ) .
```

Local equipment and machinery controls shall be arranged so as to avoid freezing, snow accumulation and ice accretion and their location to remain accessible at all time.

Original source text:
The master shall consider a route through polar waters, taking into account the following: the procedures required by the safety management system on board; if no safety management system is implemented there shall be a documented procedure for operation in polar waters; any limitations of the hydrographic information and aids to navigation available; current information on the extent and type of ice and icebergs in the vicinity of the intended route; statistical information on ice and temperatures from former years; places of refuge; current information and measures to be taken when marine mammals are encountered relating to known areas with densities of marine mammals, including seasonal migration areas; current information on relevant ships' routeing systems, speed recommendations and vessel traffic services relating to known areas with densities of marine mammals, including seasonal migration areas; national and international designated protected areas along the route; and operation in areas remote from search and rescue (SAR) capabilities.
Normalized requirement:
IF chapter_11_1_applicable = true, THEN the route planning record shall cover all nine clause 11-1.3 topics; if safety_management_system_implemented = false, documented_polar_operation_procedure_present shall be true.
Required inputs: Applicability, safety-management status, procedure artifact, and nine topic indicators

Narrow deck strips abreast of hatches and serving as ice stringers shall comply with the section modulus and shear area requirements given in 4.5.1 and 4.5.2 respectively. Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gs: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix qudtunit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gs:TRF055NarrowDeckStripShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:narrowDeckStrip ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:abreastOfHatch ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "If applicable, the narrow deck strip must be recorded as abreast of a hatch."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:servesAsIceStringer ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "If applicable, the narrow deck strip must be recorded as serving as an ice stringer."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:veryLongHatchOpening ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:actualSectionModulus ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node gs:SectionModulusQuantityShape ;
        sh:message "An actual section modulus with the required unit is required when the applicability conditions hold."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:requiredSectionModulus ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node gs:SectionModulusQuantityShape ;
        sh:message "A required section modulus with the required unit is required when the applicability conditions hold."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:actualShearArea ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node gs:ShearAreaQuantityShape ;
        sh:message "An actual shear area with the required unit is required when the applicability conditions hold."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:requiredShearArea ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node gs:ShearAreaQuantityShape ;
        sh:message "A required shear area with the required unit is required when the applicability conditions hold."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:permittedReducedLineLoad ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node gs:ReducedLineLoadQuantityShape
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:reducedLineLoadApprovedByClassificationSociety ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "When the strip is abreast of a hatch and serves as an ice stringer, the actual and required section modulus and shear area values are required." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:abreastOfHatch true ;
                      nltl:servesAsIceStringer true .

                FILTER (
                    NOT EXISTS { $this nltl:actualSectionModulus ?actualZ } ||
                    NOT EXISTS { $this nltl:requiredSectionModulus ?requiredZ } ||
                    NOT EXISTS { $this nltl:actualShearArea ?actualA } ||
                    NOT EXISTS { $this nltl:requiredShearArea ?requiredA }
                )
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "The actual section modulus and actual shear area must each be at least their corresponding required value when the applicability conditions hold." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX qudtunit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:abreastOfHatch true ;
                      nltl:servesAsIceStringer true ;
                      nltl:actualSectionModulus ?actualZ ;
                      nltl:requiredSectionModulus ?requiredZ ;
                      nltl:actualShearArea ?actualA ;
                      nltl:requiredShearArea ?requiredA .

                ?actualZ qudt:numericValue ?actualZValue ;
                         qudt:unit qudtunit:CentiM3 .
                ?requiredZ qudt:numericValue ?requiredZValue ;
                            qudt:unit qudtunit:CentiM3 .
                ?actualA qudt:numericValue ?actualAValue ;
                         qudt:unit qudtunit:CentiM2 .
                ?requiredA qudt:numericValue ?requiredAValue ;
                            qudt:unit qudtunit:CentiM2 .

                FILTER (
                    ?actualZValue < ?requiredZValue ||
                    ?actualAValue < ?requiredAValue
                )
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "A permitted reduced line load is valid only for a very long hatch, must be approved by the classification society, and must satisfy 0.10 <= p*h < 0.15 kN/m." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX qudtunit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:abreastOfHatch true ;
                      nltl:servesAsIceStringer true ;
                      nltl:permittedReducedLineLoad ?reducedLoad .

                OPTIONAL {
                    $this nltl:veryLongHatchOpening ?veryLong .
                }
                OPTIONAL {
                    $this nltl:reducedLineLoadApprovedByClassificationSociety ?approved .
                }

                OPTIONAL {
                    ?reducedLoad qudt:numericValue ?reducedValue ;
                                 qudt:unit qudtunit:KiloN-PER-M .
                }

                FILTER (
                    !BOUND(?veryLong) ||
                    ?veryLong != true ||
                    !BOUND(?approved) ||
                    ?approved != true ||
                    !BOUND(?reducedValue) ||
                    ?reducedValue < "0.10"^^xsd:decimal ||
                    ?reducedValue >= "0.15"^^xsd:decimal
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

gs:SectionModulusQuantityShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue qudtunit:CentiM3
    ] .

gs:ShearAreaQuantityShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue qudtunit:CentiM2
    ] .

        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue qudtunit:KiloN-PER-M
    ] .
```

```turtle
                sh:path nltl:permittedReducedLineLoad ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;

                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:minInclusive "0.10"^^xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:maxExclusive "0.15"^^xsd:decimal

                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:hasValue qudt:KiloN-PER-M
                    ]
                ]
            ]
        ]
    ) ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "The actual section modulus and shear area must each meet their required values." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:actualSectionModulus ?actualZ ;
                      nltl:requiredSectionModulus ?requiredZ ;
                      nltl:actualShearArea ?actualA ;
                      nltl:requiredShearArea ?requiredA .
                ?actualZ qudt:numericValue ?actualZValue ;
                         qudt:unit qudt:CentiM3 .
                ?requiredZ qudt:numericValue ?requiredZValue ;
                           qudt:unit qudt:CentiM3 .
                ?actualA qudt:numericValue ?actualAValue ;
                         qudt:unit qudt:CentiM2 .
                ?requiredA qudt:numericValue ?requiredAValue ;
                           qudt:unit qudt:CentiM2 .
                FILTER (?actualZValue < ?requiredZValue ||
                        ?actualAValue < ?requiredAValue)
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:minInclusive "0.10"^^xsd:decimal ;
        sh:maxExclusive "0.15"^^xsd:decimal
    ] .

gen:TRF055ApplicableShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:actualSectionModulus ;
        sh:node gen:SectionModulusQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [

        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [

        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:requiredShearArea ;
        sh:node gen:ShearAreaQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:designLineLoad ;

        sh:minCount 1 ;

    ] ;
    sh:property [

        sh:node gen:LengthQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipBreadth ;
        sh:node gen:LengthQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:sectionModulus ;
        sh:node gen:SectionModulusQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:scantlingApprovalStatus ;
        sh:class nltl:evidenceState ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "The actual section modulus and actual shear area shall each meet their required value." ;
        sh:select """
PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

SELECT $this
WHERE {
    $this nltl:actualSectionModulus ?actualSectionModulus .
    ?actualSectionModulus qudt:numericValue ?actualSectionModulusValue .
    $this nltl:requiredSectionModulus ?requiredSectionModulus .
    ?requiredSectionModulus qudt:numericValue ?requiredSectionModulusValue .
    $this nltl:actualShearArea ?actualShearArea .
    ?actualShearArea qudt:numericValue ?actualShearAreaValue .
    $this nltl:requiredShearArea ?requiredShearArea .
    ?requiredShearArea qudt:numericValue ?requiredShearAreaValue .
    FILTER (
        ?actualSectionModulusValue < ?requiredSectionModulusValue ||
        ?actualShearAreaValue < ?requiredShearAreaValue
    )
}
"""
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:veryLongHatchOpening ;
                sh:hasValue false
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:veryLongHatchOpening ;
                sh:hasValue true
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:permittedReducedLineLoad ;
                sh:node gen:ReducedLineLoadQuantityShape ;

                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue true
            ]
        ]
    ) .

gen:TRF055Shape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:narrowDeckStrip ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:abreastOfHatch ;
```

```turtle
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship must specify its operating area to determine whether the Arctic-waters prohibition applies."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        gen:IMO112NonArcticBranch
        gen:IMO112ArcticBranch
    ) .

gen:IMO112NonArcticBranch a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "The NLS discharge prohibition applies when the operating area is Arctic waters." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:operatingArea ?area .
                FILTER (?area = "Arctic_waters"^^xsd:string)
            }
        """
    ] .

gen:IMO112ArcticBranch a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:noxiousLiquidSubstanceOrNoxiousLiquidSubstanceMixtureDischargeToSea ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue false ;
        sh:message "Discharge of noxious liquid substances or mixtures containing them must be recorded as false in Arctic waters."
    ] .

```

```turtle
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

    nltl:hasUpperIceWaterline ex:upperIceWaterline .

    nltl:longitudinalPosition ex:i1X1 ;
    nltl:verticalCoordinate ex:i1Z1 .

    nltl:verticalCoordinate ex:i1Z2 .

    qudt:unit unit:M .

    qudt:unit unit:M .

ex:i1Z1 a qudt:QuantityValue ;

    qudt:numericValue 4.5 ;
    qudt:unit unit:M .

        ex:i1Point2 .

        ex:upperPoint2 .
```

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix q: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-011-ShipShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasIntendedIceOperatingWaterline ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:iceWaterline ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-011-IceWaterlineShape> ;
        sh:message "The ship must identify its intended ice-operating waterline and its profile points."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasUpperIceWaterline ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:iceWaterline ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-011-IceWaterlineShape> ;
        sh:message "The ship must identify an upper ice waterline and its profile points."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "The upper ice waterline must equal, pointwise, the envelope of the highest intended ice-operating waterline points at each longitudinal position." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX q: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                {
                    SELECT $this ?x
                           (MAX(?iy) AS ?maxY)
                           (MIN(?uy) AS ?minUpperY)
                           (MAX(?uy) AS ?maxUpperY)
                           (COUNT(?ip) AS ?intendedCount)
                           (COUNT(?up) AS ?upperCount)
                    WHERE {
                        {
                            $this nltl:hasIntendedIceOperatingWaterline ?iw .
                            ?iw nltl:hasWaterlineProfilePoint ?ip .
                            ?ip nltl:longitudinalPosition ?ixq .
                            ?ixq q:numericValue ?x .
                            ?ip nltl:verticalCoordinate ?iyq .
                            ?iyq q:numericValue ?iy .
                        }
                        UNION
                        {
                            $this nltl:hasUpperIceWaterline ?uw .
                            ?uw nltl:hasWaterlineProfilePoint ?up .
                            ?up nltl:longitudinalPosition ?uxq .
                            ?uxq q:numericValue ?x .
                            ?up nltl:verticalCoordinate ?uyq .
                            ?uyq q:numericValue ?uy .
                        }
                    }
                    GROUP BY $this ?x
                }
                FILTER (
                    ?intendedCount = 0 ||
                    ?upperCount = 0 ||
                    ?minUpperY != ?maxY ||
                    ?maxUpperY != ?maxY
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-011-IceWaterlineShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class nltl:iceWaterline ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasWaterlineProfilePoint ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:waterlineProfilePoint ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-011-ProfilePointShape> ;
        sh:message "Each ice waterline must contain profile points."
    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-011-ProfilePointShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class nltl:waterlineProfilePoint ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:longitudinalPosition ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-011-LengthQuantityShape> ;
        sh:message "Each waterline profile point must have one longitudinal position in metres."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:verticalCoordinate ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-011-LengthQuantityShape> ;

    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-011-LengthQuantityShape>
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .

gen:TRF011_ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasIntendedIceOperatingWaterline ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:iceWaterline ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:TRF011_IceWaterlineShape ;
        sh:message "The ship must identify the intended ice-operating waterline and its profile points."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasUpperIceWaterline ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:iceWaterline ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:TRF011_IceWaterlineShape ;
        sh:message "The ship must identify the upper ice waterline and its profile points."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "The upper ice waterline must match, within a relative tolerance of 1e-6 plus an absolute fallback of 1e-9 m, the maximum vertical coordinate of intended ice-operating waterline points at every longitudinal position, with no missing or extra pointwise positions." ;
        sh:select """
PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

SELECT DISTINCT ?this
WHERE {
  ?this a nltl:ship ;
        nltl:hasIntendedIceOperatingWaterline ?intended ;
        nltl:hasUpperIceWaterline ?upper .

  {
    SELECT ?x
           (MAX(?intendedValue) AS ?expectedValue)
           (MIN(?upperValue) AS ?upperMinimum)
           (MAX(?upperValue) AS ?upperMaximum)
    WHERE {
      {
        ?this nltl:hasIntendedIceOperatingWaterline ?intendedLine .
        ?intendedLine nltl:hasWaterlineProfilePoint ?intendedPoint .
        ?intendedPoint nltl:longitudinalPosition ?intendedPosition ;
                        nltl:verticalCoordinate ?intendedCoordinate .
        ?intendedPosition qudt:numericValue ?x .
        ?intendedCoordinate qudt:numericValue ?intendedValue .
      }
      UNION
      {
        ?this nltl:hasUpperIceWaterline ?upperLine .
        ?upperLine nltl:hasWaterlineProfilePoint ?upperPoint .
        ?upperPoint nltl:longitudinalPosition ?upperPosition ;
                    nltl:verticalCoordinate ?upperCoordinate .
        ?upperPosition qudt:numericValue ?x .
        ?upperCoordinate qudt:numericValue ?upperValue .
      }
    }
    GROUP BY ?x
  }

  FILTER (
    !BOUND(?expectedValue) ||
    !BOUND(?upperMinimum) ||
    !BOUND(?upperMaximum) ||
    ABS(?upperMinimum - ?expectedValue) >
      (1e-6 * ABS(?expectedValue) + 1e-9) ||
    ABS(?upperMaximum - ?expectedValue) >
      (1e-6 * ABS(?expectedValue) + 1e-9)
  )
}
"""
    ] .

gen:TRF011_IceWaterlineShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:iceWaterline ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasWaterlineProfilePoint ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:waterlineProfilePoint ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:TRF011_WaterlineProfilePointShape ;
        sh:message "Each ice waterline used by this requirement must contain at least one waterline profile point."
    ] .

gen:TRF011_WaterlineProfilePointShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:waterlineProfilePoint ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:longitudinalPosition ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:TRF011_LengthQuantityShape ;
        sh:message "Each waterline profile point must provide one longitudinal position in metres."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:verticalCoordinate ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:TRF011_LengthQuantityShape ;
        sh:message "Each waterline profile point must provide one vertical coordinate in metres."
    ] .

gen:TRF011_LengthQuantityShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue qudt:M
    ] .
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:TRF011ShipShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasIntendedIceOperatingWaterline ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:iceWaterline ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:TRF011IceWaterlineShape ;
        sh:message "The ship must identify its intended ice-operating waterline with profile points."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasUpperIceWaterline ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:iceWaterline ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:TRF011IceWaterlineShape ;
        sh:message "The ship must identify an upper ice waterline with profile points."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "The upper ice waterline must equal, within a relative tolerance of 1e-6 plus an absolute fallback of 1e-9 m near zero, the maximum vertical coordinate of the intended ice-operating waterline at every longitudinal position, with no unmatched positions." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                {
                    SELECT $this ?x
                           (MAX(?iv) AS ?im)
                           (MAX(?uv) AS ?um)
                           (COUNT(?iv) AS ?ic)
                           (COUNT(?uv) AS ?uc)
                    WHERE {
                        {
                            $this nltl:hasIntendedIceOperatingWaterline ?iw .
                            ?iw nltl:hasWaterlineProfilePoint ?ip .
                            ?ip nltl:longitudinalPosition ?xq ;
                                nltl:verticalCoordinate ?vq .
                            ?xq qudt:numericValue ?x ;
                                qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> .
                            ?vq qudt:numericValue ?v ;
                                qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> .
                            BIND(?v AS ?iv)
                        }
                        UNION
                        {
                            $this nltl:hasUpperIceWaterline ?uw .
                            ?uw nltl:hasWaterlineProfilePoint ?up .
                            ?up nltl:longitudinalPosition ?uxq ;
                                nltl:verticalCoordinate ?uvq .
                            ?uxq qudt:numericValue ?x ;
                                qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> .
                            ?uvq qudt:numericValue ?uv ;
                                qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> .
                        }
                    }
                    GROUP BY $this ?x
                }
                FILTER(
                    ?ic = 0 ||
                    ?uc = 0 ||
                    ABS(?im - ?um) >
                    (1e-6 * ABS(?im) + 1e-9)
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

gen:TRF011IceWaterlineShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasWaterlineProfilePoint ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:waterlineProfilePoint ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:TRF011ProfilePointShape ;
        sh:message "Each represented ice waterline must contain profile points."
    ] .

gen:TRF011ProfilePointShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:longitudinalPosition ;

        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:TRF011LengthQuantityShape ;
        sh:message "Each waterline profile point must have one vertical coordinate in metres."
    ] .

gen:TRF011LengthQuantityShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> ;
```

F. MANUAL REVIEW CHECKLIST — DO NOT PREFILL GRADES
Q1 Applicability: Does the shape activate for the correct ships, conditions, and scope?
Q2 Ownership / paths: Are target ownership and property paths correct?
Q3 Required constraints: Are all mandatory obligations represented?
Q4 Units / calculation: Are quantities, units, formulae, and comparisons correct?
Q5 Branches / boundaries: Are alternatives, exceptions, equality boundaries, and conditionals correct?
Q6 No extra restrictions: Does the shape avoid requirements not supported by the source?
Q7 Traceability / clarity: Is the implementation understandable and traceable to the cited evidence?
Record grades, reviewer identity, evidence, and notes in the workbook. Do not infer full-requirement correctness from these two cases.
